1	\paragraph{The epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
2	The epoch confound identified in \S\ref{sec:main-results} accounts for ${>}$95\% of D\&R's reported advantage; the residual ($\Delta{=}0.017$, 3 seeds) falls within cross-seed variance and is robust to LR schedule choice ($\Delta{=}0.006$ under independent schedules).
3	A permuted-score control ($+$0.26 vs.\ uniform) confirms that detector signal is load-bearing.
4	
5	\paragraph{When uniform beats reweighting.}
6	Beyond the evaluation confound, our results identify three operational conditions for effective reweighting, each of which, when violated, makes uniform training preferable.
7	First, \emph{detector quality}: the detector must provide both sufficient discrimination (AUC) and good calibration (ECE).
8	A high-AUC but poorly calibrated detector (GPT-2, ECE\,=\,0.131) degrades PPL by $+$0.098 below uniform; even a well-calibrated detector with insufficient discrimination (N-gram, AUC\,=\,0.73) degrades by $+$0.129 (\S\ref{sec:robustness}).
9	Second, \emph{contamination level}: at $\rho{=}0.2$, the detector's signal-to-noise ratio is too low and the method actively intervenes on unreliable signal ($\aeff$-binding at 60.1\% despite mean $q{=}0.393$), degrading PPL by $+$0.046; the method transitions from harmful to beneficial with a sharp sign change in $[0.3, 0.4]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:delta_vs_rho}, Appendix~\ref{app:rho03}).
10	Third, \emph{scale}: at 1.7B the benefit diminishes ($\Delta{=}{-}0.013$) with OOD partially inconsistent, suggesting that larger models may be inherently more robust to contamination generated by smaller models.
11	In all three failure cases, the adaptive method's dual constraint limits degradation relative to unconstrained reweighting (ESS-only), but uniform remains the safer default when conditions are uncertain (Table~\ref{tab:decision}).

1	\subsection{Main Results}
2	\label{sec:main-results}
3	
4	Table~\ref{tab:main} reports held-out perplexity at $\rho = 0.4$ across three seeds.
5	Continued pretraining reduces PPL from 22.970 to the 17.3--17.8 range; method comparisons operate within a narrow residual.
6	
7	Adaptive $\bstar$ achieves 3-seed mean PPL 17.674, compared to 17.694 for uniform ($\Delta{=}{-}0.020$, directionally consistent across all seeds) and 17.620 for grid $b{=}0.3$ (3-seed mean; gap $+$0.054).
8	A paired $t$-test yields $t{=}{-}7.1$, $p{=}0.019$; after Bonferroni--Holm correction for nine family-wise comparisons, $p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.078$, which does not reach conventional significance, though the 95\% BCa bootstrap CI $[{-}0.028, {-}0.012]$ excludes zero.
9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.
10	The grid-best $b{=}0.3$ is a post-hoc oracle from nine candidates with $4.7{\times}$ higher cross-seed standard deviation than adaptive ($\sigma{=}0.033$ vs.\ $0.007$); the noisy PPL plateau at $b \in [0.1, 0.5]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:grid_b_trend}) makes validation-based selection unreliable.
11	At the nearest fixed point ($b{=}1.0$), PPL equals uniform (17.692 vs.\ 17.694): per-batch adaptation is essential.
12	
13	The ablation hierarchy at $\rho{=}0.4$ (Adaptive ${<}$ Uniform ${<}$ ESS-only ${<}$ Random, Table~\ref{tab:main}) reveals two requirements for effective reweighting: detector signal and minimum intervention.
14	Random weights $\mathcal{U}(0,2)$ (17.802) confirm that detector $q$-scores carry genuine provenance signal; uninformed reweighting only increases gradient variance.
15	ESS-only (mean $17.737 {\pm} 0.021$) uses the same $q$-scores but maximizes $b$ up to the stability ceiling, concentrating weights on a few high-confidence samples; it degrades PPL by 0.043 relative to uniform.

1	Let $\mathcal{D} = \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{real}} \cup \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}$ denote a pretraining corpus with token-level contamination ratio $\rho = |\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}| / |\mathcal{D}|$, assumed unknown~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	A detector assigns each sample $x$ a synthetic probability $q(x) \in [0,1]$; following \citet{drayson2025detection}, we weight each sample as $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^{b}$, where $b \geq 0$ is a sharpening exponent ($b{=}0$ recovers uniform training).
3	Rather than selecting $b$ via grid search~\citep{drayson2025detection}, we derive $b$ per-batch from the batch's $q$-score distribution using two opposing constraints.
4	
5	Given a batch $\mathcal{B} = \{x_1, \ldots, x_B\}$ with scores $q_i = q(x_i)$ and weights $w_i(b) = (1 - q_i)^{b}$:
6	
7	\paragraph{Quality floor.}
8	The \emph{effective authenticity} $\aeff(b) = \sum_i w_i(b)(1{-}q_i) / \sum_i w_i(b)$ measures the weighted mean detector confidence that samples are real.
9	We require $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$, yielding a lower bound $b_\tau$ on the exponent, motivated by convergence theory under contamination~\citep{wang2026survive}.
10	$\aeff$ correlates with the true batch-level real fraction at Pearson $r \approx 0.79$ (Appendix~\ref{app:alpha_validation}); limitations are discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}.
11	
12	\paragraph{Stability ceiling.}
13	Kish's effective sample size $\mathrm{ESS}(b) = (\sum_i w_i)^2 / \sum_i w_i^2$~\citep{kish1965survey} decreases monotonically in $b$ (Appendix~\ref{app:proofs}).
14	We require $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$, yielding an upper bound $\bess$.
15	
16	\paragraph{Adaptive $\bstar$.}
17	Since $\aeff$ is non-decreasing and ESS is decreasing in $b$, the two constraints define an interval $[b_\tau, \bess]$.
18	The optimal exponent implements \emph{minimum intervention}:
19	\begin{equation}
20	  b^{*} = \begin{cases}
21	    0        & \text{if } \aeff(0) \geq \tau, \\
22	    b_\tau   & \text{if } b_\tau \leq \bess, \\
23	    \bess & \tex
…[truncated 632 chars]

395	\section{Cross-Model Transfer}
396	\label{app:cross_model}
397	
398	Table~\ref{tab:cross_model} reports a single exploratory run with Llama-3.2-3B as a cross-generator (synthetic data generated by Llama, training on Qwen3-0.6B); this does not establish generality.
399	Results are mixed: MMLU improves while HellaSwag slightly degrades, consistent with the pattern observed in Qwen3-1.7B downstream evaluation (\S\ref{sec:robustness}).
400	Perplexity comparison omitted due to inconsistent evaluation configurations; checkpoints were not retained for re-evaluation.

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_10/projects/provenance_weight_training/docs/paper/sections/introduction.tex:11:To ground the analysis of exponent selection, we implement a dual-constrained formulation as an illustrative application: find the minimum $b$ such that $\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}(b) \geq \tau$ (quality floor, motivated by convergence theory~\citep{wang2026survive}), subject to $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq n_{\min}$ (stability ceiling)~\citep{kish1965survey,vehtari2024psis}.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_10/projects/provenance_weight_training/docs/paper/sections/conclusion.tex:2:As an engineering demonstration of the minimum-intervention principle, a dual-constrained adaptive $\bstar$ improves over uniform at $\rho \geq 0.4$ (directionally consistent across all seeds; $\rho{=}0.6$: uncorrected $p{=}0.010$) without tuning; batch-size amplification is monotonic across $B \in \{4, 8, 16\}$ ($1.0{\times}$ to $3.4{\times}$).

9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.
10	The grid-best $b{=}0.3$ is a post-hoc oracle from nine candidates with $4.7{\times}$ higher cross-seed standard deviation than adaptive ($\sigma{=}0.033$ vs.\ $0.007$); the noisy PPL plateau at $b \in [0.1, 0.5]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:grid_b_trend}) makes validation-based selection unreliable.
11	At the nearest fixed point ($b{=}1.0$), PPL equals uniform (17.692 vs.\ 17.694): per-batch adaptation is essential.

122	The coupling $\nmin{=}\lfloor B/2 \rfloor$ is also empirically motivated: at $B{=}8$, keeping $\nmin{=}2$ (ratio $0.25$) degrades PPL by $+$0.036 vs.\ uniform, whereas $\nmin{=}4$ (ratio $0.5$) yields $\Delta{=}{-}0.052{\pm}0.007$.
123	Under this coupling, the adaptive advantage monotonically increases across $B \in \{4, 8, 16\}$: $\Delta{=}{-}0.020{\pm}0.006$ (3 seeds), $-$0.052${\pm}$0.007 (3 seeds, $p{=}0.006$), and $-$0.068${\pm}$0.005 (3 seeds), corresponding to $1.0{\times}$, $2.6{\times}$, and $3.4{\times}$ amplification ($p{<}0.01$ at $B \geq 8$).
124	The gap to grid-best narrows from 0.040 ($B{=}4$) to 0.009 ($B{=}16$, seed\,42).

20	
21	\item \textbf{Minimum intervention outperforms maximization.}
22	An $\aeff$ quality constraint binds in ${\sim}$50\% of batches at ${\approx}5.9{\times}$ lower sharpening exponents than an ESS-only alternative (\S\ref{sec:regime}); at $\rho{=}0.6$, ESS-only collapses toward uniform while the dual constraint maintains its advantage.
23	\end{itemize}
24	To demonstrate the minimum-intervention principle, we derive a dual-constrained adaptive $\bstar$ that eliminates per-model grid search ($\Delta{=}{-}0.020$ at $\rho{=}0.4$, $-$0.032 at $\rho{=}0.6$; Table~\ref{tab:main}), with transferable defaults ($\tau{=}0.7$, $\nmin{=}\lfloor B/2 \rfloor$).

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Sample a random subset from scored_data.jsonl to match D&R retention count.\\n\\nCreates a random-selection baseline: same number of samples as D&R q<=0.3,\\nbut chosen randomly instead of by quality score. This isolates the effect\\nof multi-epoch repetition from filtering quality.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport random\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--input\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--n_samples\\\", type=int, default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Exact number of samples to keep\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--ratio\\\", type=float, default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Fraction of samples to keep (alternative to n_samples)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    lines = []\\n    with open(args.input) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                lines.append(line)\\n\\n    total = len(lines)\\n    if args.n_samples is not None:\\n        n = args.n_samples\\n    elif args.ratio is not None:\\n        n = int(total * args.ratio)\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Must specify --n_samples or --ratio\\\")\\n\\n    random.seed(args.seed)\\n    selected = random.sample(range(total), n)\\n    selected.sort()\\n\\n    with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for idx in selected:\\n            f.write(lines[idx])\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Total: {total}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Sampled: {n} ({n/total*100:.2f}%)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Seed: {args.seed}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Output: {args.output}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"}

2	  "raw": "8 个文件全部生成（4 figure × pdf/png）。\n\n---\n\n## 修改汇总\n\n### Fix 1: Fig 2 constraint_regime_decomposition — τ=0.8 → τ=0.7\n| 字段 | ρ=0.4 旧值 (τ=0.8) | ρ=0.4 新值 (τ=0.7) | ρ=0.6 旧值 | ρ=0.6 新值 |\n|------|-----|-----|-----|-----|\n| α_eff binding | 38.2% | **50.9%** | 33.4% | **46.8%** |\n| ESS binding | 59.8% | **43.3%** | 66.0% | **48.7%** |\n| Clean | 2.0% | **5.8%** | 0.6% | **4.5%** |\n\n数据来源：τ=0.7 3-seed (seed42/123/456) step_metrics.jsonl 实时提取\n\n### Fix 2: Fig 4 dr_dual_curve — adaptive 参考线\n- 旧值：**17.651**（实际是 grid b=0.5 seed42 的 PPL）\n- 新值：**17.674**（τ=0.7 3-seed adaptive mean = (17.680+17.666+17.677)/3）\n\n### Fix 3: Fig 3 τ=0.8 PPL — 保持 17.6527\n- 脚本值 17.6527 来自 `adaptive_fix_seed42`（n_min=2 修正版）\n- Registry 中 17.644 来自 `adaptive_seed42`（n_min=3 原始版，有 bug）\n- tau sweep 其他数据点均使用相同实验体系，**17.6527 是一致且正确的值**，不修改\n\n### 生成文件路径\n```\n/root/provenance_weight_training/output/figures/\n├── constraint_regime_decomposition.pdf  (22KB)\n├── constraint_regime_decomposition.png  (53KB)\n├── dr_dual_curve.pdf                    (29KB)\n├── dr_dual_curve.png                    (103KB)\n├── grid_b_trend_clean.pdf               (21KB)\n├── grid_b_trend_clean.png               (88KB)\n├── tau_rho_rank_preservation.pdf         (27KB)\n└── tau_rho_rank_preservation.png         (118KB)\n```\n\n### 验证清单\n- [x] Fig 2 柱状图数据 ≈ 50.9/43.3/5.8（ρ=0.4）\n- [x] Fig 4 adaptive 参考线 = 17.674\n- [x] Fig 3 τ=0.8 数据点 = 17.6527（与 adaptive_fix 一致，保持不变）\n- [x] 所有 figure 重新生成成功"

126	
127	\paragraph{Constraint regime.}
128	\label{sec:regime}
129	Over 24{,}412 micro-batches (seed\,42), 5.8\% are clean ($\aeff(0) \geq \tau$, $\bstar{=}0$), ${\sim}$50\% are $\aeff$-binding (quality constraint dominates), and ${\sim}$44\% are ESS-binding (stability constraint dominates); the distribution is stable across seeds (CV ${<}2\%$; Appendix~\ref{app:regime}).
130	The $\aeff$-binding majority indicates that detector quality signal---not training stability---is the primary bottleneck, reinforcing the calibration requirement (\S\ref{sec:robustness}).

130	We tested a per-batch adaptive variant that clamps $\tau$ between 0.5 and the fixed threshold based on local $\aeff(0)$, allowing the quality floor to relax in batches with low estimated real fraction.

96	Random remains substantially worse than uniform (gap 0.098, $\sigma{=}0.025$): detector signal is load-bearing even when the stability constraint dominates; the adaptive method's continued advantage is driven by the $\aeff$ quality constraint, which operates independently of the ESS ceiling.

[severity: warning] `permuted_score_control_seed42` 使用 τ=0.8，与主实验 τ=0.7 不匹配**\n\nspec 显示 `--tau 0.8`，但主实验（adaptive_fix_seed42 等所有 N=5 seeds）均使用 `--tau 0.7`。Step metrics 确认 tau_batch=0.8 从 step 0 开始。\n\n作为 score 排列控制实验，应与 treatment 组使用完全相同的超参数（除 score 排列外），否则引入混淆变量。τ=0.8 触发更多 batch 进入 reweighting（α_eff < 0.8 的 batch 更多），对比不公。\n\n**建议**：实验仍在 step 69/6103，损失极小。建议 kill 后以 `--tau 0.7` 重启。\n\n**2. [severity: warning] `p0_batchsize_16` 注册表状态陈旧**\n\n注册表显示 `p0_batchsize_16` 为 RUNNING，但 Worker 已报告 OOM（18.55 GiB logits tensor 超出 GPU 剩余显存）。GPU 1 上实际运行的是 `p0_nmin_5`（PID 963054，cmdline 确认 `--output_dir .../p0_nmin_5 --n_min 5`）。同时 `p0_nmin_5` 注册表状态仍为 PREPARING。\n\n需更新：p0_batchsize_16 → FAILED (OOM)，p0_nmin_5 → RUNNING (pid=963054)。\n\n**3. [severity: info] `p0_nmin_5` 功能上与 nmin4_seed42 重复**\n\nn_min=5 > B=4 → ESS ≥ 5 对 batch_size=4 不可能满足 → b*=0 始终成立。Step metrics 确认 b=0.0, ess=4.0。nmin4_seed42 已展示完全相同行为（b*=0 100%, PPL=17.6947 ≈ uniform 17.694）。\n\n作为 n_min 灵敏度曲线的边界点可接受，但不会产生新信息。如果 GPU 时间紧张可考虑让出。\n\n**4. [severity: info] `detect_remove_q05_seed42` 近期重启**\n\n注册表 PID=601609（2026-05-21 创建），实际 PID=962199。当前 step ~120/6103，~2.08s/it，已运行约 4 分钟。说明近期（可能在服务器重启后）刚重新启动。训练正常进行中。\n\n数据 153725 docs（41.4% 保留），用 uniform 权重训练 full 6103 steps。与之前 `dr_epoch_matched_indep_lr_seed42`（epoch-matched, PPL=18.78）设计不同——这次给 filtered 数据 full token budget（multi-epoch），更公平的 D&R hard-filtering baseline。\n\n**5. [severity: info] 新实验均未在 experiment_plan.yaml 中注册**\n\n`permuted_score_control_seed42` 和 `detect_remove_q05_seed42` 没有对应的 experiment_plan entry（后者映射的是旧的 dr_epoch_matched 实验）。建议在 plan 中补充 entry 以便覆盖率追踪。\n\n---\n\n**结论: WARNING**（τ=0.8 confound + 注册表状态陈旧）\n\n**优先行动建议**：\n1. **P0**: Kill `permuted_score_control_seed42`，改 `--tau 0.7` 重启（仅损失 ~4 分钟训练）\n2. **P0**: 更新注册表 — p0_batchsize_16 → FAILED, p0_nmin_5 → RUNNING",

  "text": "## 审查报告 — CRITICAL: 磁盘满 + 连锁实验崩溃\n\n**[severity: critical] /root/autodl-tmp 磁盘 100% 已满 (250G/250G, 仅剩 200K)**\n\n### 已发生的事故\n\n1. **p0_nmin_5** 在 step 500（首次 checkpoint save）崩溃：\n   ```\n   RuntimeError: [enforce fail at inline_container.cc:668] . unexpected pos 86016 vs 85908\n   ```\n   torch.save optimizer state 失败。GPU 1 现已空闲 (0%, 0 MiB)。\n\n2. **detect_remove_q05_seed42** 旧进程 (PID 601613, May 21 启动) 在 step 5827/6103 (95.5%) 被 SIGKILL（EXIT_CODE=137）。cron on_failed 触发的是这个旧进程。**新进程 (PID 962199) 仍在 GPU 2 上运行，step ~499。**\n\n### 即将发生的崩溃（紧急）\n\n所有 3 个存活实验将在下一个 checkpoint save 崩溃：\n\n| 实验 | GPU | 当前 step | 下次 save | 距崩溃 |\n|------|-----|-----------|-----------|--------|\n| random_subset_epoch_seed42 | 0 | ~3904/6103 | step 4000 | **~3 分钟** |\n| detect_remove_q05_seed42 (new) | 2 | ~499/6103 | step 500 | **即将** |\n| permuted_score_control_seed42 | 3 | ~500+/6103 | step 500/1000 | **即将/已崩** |\n\n### 根因\n\nmodels/ 目录占 **227G**。大量 DONE 实验保留了 2 个完整 checkpoint（各 3.4G = 6.8G/实验）+ final（1.2G）= 7.9G/实验。已评估完毕的实验不需要保留 checkpoint。\n\n粗估可回收空间：~20 个 DONE 实验 × 6.7G checkpoint = **~130G**。\n\n### 紧急行动建议（按优先级）\n\n1. **立即**: 删除已评估 DONE 实验的 checkpoint-* 目录（保留 final/），释放 ~130G。需要 Worker 用 ssh_execute 执行。以下实验安全删除 checkpoint：\n   - uniform_seed789, uniform_seed234, adaptive_seed789, adaptive_seed234\n   - bs8_nmin4_* (6 个), rho03_* 系列, aeff_only_* (3 个)\n   - 以及其他 DONE 且已评估的实验\n\n2. **同时**: 检查 random_subset_epoch_seed42 是否已在 step 4000 崩溃。如果还活着，磁盘清理完后它可以继续。\n\n3. **之后**: 重启 p0_nmin_5（如果仍需要——此实验功能上与 nmin4_seed42 重复）。\n\n4. **之后**: 检查 detect_remove 和 permuted_score 是否存活或需要重启。\n\n**注意**: detect_remove_q05_seed42 的旧 checkpoint（5500/5000/4500, May 21）来自旧进程。如果旧进程的训练是有效的，可以从 checkpoint-5500 resume 而非从头开始，节省 ~3 小时。但需确认旧进程的命令与新进程是否兼容。",

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — p0_nmin_5\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **实验设计退化：n_min=5 > B=4 导致 adaptive weighting 数学上等价于 uniform。** 日志中从 step 0 到 step 500（crash 前全部记录），`weighting/b` **恒等于 0**，`weighting/ess` **恒等于 4**（= B），`unweighted_loss == weighted_loss` 全程成立。原因：ESS 约束要求 ESS ≥ n_min=5，但 batch size B=4 时 ESS 的最大值就是 B=4（当所有权重相等时取到），5 > 4 永远不可能满足，b* 被强制为 0。**这不是\"更频繁地退化为 uniform\"，而是 100% 保证退化为 uniform。** 即使重跑成功，结果也会与已有的 uniform baseline（如 uniform_seed42, holdout_ppl=17.694）在噪声范围内一致，**不提供新信息**。如果要测试 n_min=5 的 sensitivity，必须使用 B ≥ n_min（例如 B=8 配 n_min=5）。\n\n- [severity: info] **Crash 根因是磁盘 I/O 错误，非\"进程多次死亡\"。** 日志明确显示 step 500 保存 checkpoint 时 `RuntimeError: basic_ios::clear: iostream error` → `RuntimeError: [enforce fail at inline_container.cc:668] unexpected pos 86016 vs 85908`（torch 写 optimizer.pt 失败）。Registry 记录的 failure_reason \"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed\" 是 auto-monitor 的误判，真实原因是磁盘写入异常。当前 `/root/autodl-tmp` 使用率 90%（224G/250G）。同时间 `permuted_score_control_seed42` 也在同一服务器 crash，可能是同一磁盘问题。\n\n- [severity: info] **GPU 合规**：cuda:1 在项目分配范围（cuda:0-3）内，无越界。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. **不要重跑 p0_nmin_5**——即使成功也是 uniform 的冗余复制。如果 n_min sensitivity curve 需要 n_min=5 这个数据点，应在 B=8 配置下测试（B=8, n_min=5），或将当前 B=4 实验的 n_min=4 结论直接标注为\"n_min ≥ B 时退化为 uniform\"的理论边界。\n2. 清理 `/root/autodl-tmp` 上的旧 checkpoint（已 90% 满），防止后续实验再次 crash。\n3. 修正 registry 中 p0_nmin_5 的 failure_reason 为真实原因（disk I/O error at step 500）。",

    "text": "## 审查报告 — p0_nmin_5\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **实验设计退化：n_min=5 > B=4 导致 adaptive weighting 数学上等价于 uniform。** 日志中从 step 0 到 step 500（crash 前全部记录），`weighting/b` **恒等于 0**，`weighting/ess` **恒等于 4**（= B），`unweighted_loss == weighted_loss` 全程成立。原因：ESS 约束要求 ESS ≥ n_min=5，但 batch size B=4 时 ESS 的最大值就是 B=4（当所有权重相等时取到），5 > 4 永远不可能满足，b* 被强制为 0。**这不是\"更频繁地退化为 uniform\"，而是 100% 保证退化为 uniform。** 即使重跑成功，结果也会与已有的 uniform baseline（如 uniform_seed42, holdout_ppl=17.694）在噪声范围内一致，**不提供新信息**。如果要测试 n_min=5 的 sensitivity，必须使用 B ≥ n_min（例如 B=8 配 n_min=5）。\n\n- [severity: info] **Crash 根因是磁盘 I/O 错误，非\"进程多次死亡\"。** 日志明确显示 step 500 保存 checkpoint 时 `RuntimeError: basic_ios::clear: iostream error` → `RuntimeError: [enforce fail at inline_container.cc:668] unexpected pos 86016 vs 85908`（torch 写 optimizer.pt 失败）。Registry 记录的 failure_reason \"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed\" 是 auto-monitor 的误判，真实原因是磁盘写入异常。当前 `/root/autodl-tmp` 使用率 90%（224G/250G）。同时间 `permuted_score_control_seed42` 也在同一服务器 crash，可能是同一磁盘问题。\n\n- [severity: info] **GPU 合规**：cuda:1 在项目分配范围（cuda:0-3）内，无越界。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. **不要重跑 p0_nmin_5**——即使成功也是 uniform 的冗余复制。如果 n_min sensitivity curve 需要 n_min=5 这个数据点，应在 B=8 配置下测试（B=8, n_min=5），或将当前 B=4 实验的 n_min=4 结论直接标注为\"n_min ≥ B 时退化为 uniform\"的理论边界。\n2. 清理 `/root/autodl-tmp` 上的旧 checkpoint（已 90% 满），防止后续实验再次 crash。\n3. 修正 registry 中 p0_nmin_5 的 failure_reason 为真实原因（disk I/O error at step 500）。",
    "project": "provenance_weight_training"
  }
}
